White: Coolant leak 
Blue: Oil burning 
▪ Phase Rotation 
Phase Sequence: ABC (clockwise) or ACB (counter-clockwise) - must match the installation diagram |  | Good | Not Good

   - Customer
2. **Informasi Customer & Equipment (Metadata):**
   - Service type | TRUE | Preventive Maintenance | FALSE | Corrective Maintenance | FALSE | Warranty | Prepared | Checked | Reviewed | Approved
   - Customer
   - Company Name * : | Neutra DC Cikarang | Product Years  * : | 2021 | MOP No | : DME-TDE/MOP/Lighting/01 2702/26
   - Class ID * : | Lampu Indoor Office | Product Name * : | PHILIPS | Date | :
   - e. | Make sure to use lights with the same color temperature | e. | The light color is uniform across the same area.

3. **Tabel Kegiatan / Parameter / Pengukuran:**
   - *Header Kolom:* `1. | Visual Inspection`
   - *Header Kolom:* `a. | Visual inspection of the lamp by switching on the lamp and whether the lamp is not bright/damaged | a. | Lights are functioning normally, not dim or damaged`
   - *Header Kolom:* `b. | Regular visual inspection of all lighting fixtures to ensure they are in good working condition. | b. | All fixtures are clean, securely installed, and functioning properly.`
   - *Header Kolom:* `h. | Perform visual inspection of the light panel components (contactor, relay, terminal & MCB) | h. | There are no signs of damage, burning, or worn component`
   - *Header Kolom:* `a. Measuring the 220V input power supply (Phase - Neutral) using AC/DC Clamp Meter | a. | Voltage (V), 230V (1-phase) ± 10% (normal operation) for 50Hz system. IEC 60038 (Standard voltages)`
4. **Catatan & Tanda Tangan (Signatures / Approvals):**
   - Format standar signature di baris akhir.
